 主席報告



Dear Shareholders,                                                     各位股東：

On behalf of the Board of Directors of China Risun Group Limited,      本人謹代表中國旭陽集團有限公司董事會，
I am pleased to present our first annual report of the Company         欣然呈報本公司及其附屬公司截至 2018 年
and its subsidiaries for the year ended December 31, 2018.             12 月 31 日 止 年 度 的 首 份 年 報。本 公 司 自
The Company listed on the Main Board of the Hong Kong Stock            2019 年 3 月 15 日起於香港聯交所主板上市。
Exchange since March 15, 2019.

REVIEW                                                                 回顧
Year of 2018 was a complex one with challenges and                     2018 年是錯綜複雜的一年，充滿挑戰，亦
achievements. Challenges are those political turmoil, unstable         不乏成就。挑戰在於世界政局動盪不定，經
economic growth and intensified economic and trade frictions           濟增長不穩，中美經貿摩擦加劇。相反，我
between China and USA. On the contrary, we continued to                們繼續努力提高焦炭平均售價，優化生產
endeavor to increase our average selling price of coke and optimize    成本，充分發揮環保效益，抓住行業整合帶
production cost, gave into full play the benefits of environmental     來的機遇，通過營運管理服務拓展業務，從
protection, as well as capturing the opportunities that emerged        而提高營運利潤率及其可持續性。因此，我
due to industry integration and expanding our business through         們於 2018 年歸屬於本公司擁有人的淨利潤
operation management services, thereby improved operation profit       創歷史新高，約為人民幣 2,088.7 百萬元，
margin and its sustainability. As such, our net profit attributable    較去年增加約 176.8%。
to owners of the Company in 2018 achieved a new historical high
amounting to approximately RMB2,088.7 million, representing an
increase of approximately 176.8% as compared to previous year.

In the past since establishment in 1995, we have established           自 1995 年創立以來，我們已涉足焦炭和精
nearly 24 years in coke and refined chemical industry. By mean         細化工行業近 24 年。憑藉縱向一體化業務
of vertically integrated business model, highly automation             模式、高度自動化和信息化系統、先進環保
and information systems, advanced environmental protection             措施、四個生產園區附近的綜合運輸基礎設
measures, comprehensive transportation infrastructure near our         施，我們繼續加強與中國其他焦炭企業競爭
four production bases, we continued strengthening our competitive      的優勢，將自身定位為全球最大的獨立焦炭
advantage amongst other coke enterprises in China and positioned       生產商及供應商，並於中國或全球的多個
ourselves as the world’s largest independent coke producer and         精細化工產品領域處於領先地位。因此，我
supplier and leading roles in a number of refined chemical sectors     們的企業在焦炭和精細化工行業享負盛譽。
in China or globally. Therefore, we have a high corporate reputation
in the coke and refined chemical industry.

KEY COMPETITIVE STRENGTHS                                              主要競爭優勢
Our vertically integrated business model helps to (i) improve          我們的縱向一體化業務模式有助 (i) 透過集中
production efficiency and achieve synergies through centralized        統一管理提高生產效率及實現協同效應，(ii)
and unified management, (ii) reduce exposure to market volatility      減少面臨的市場波動及價格波動風險，(iii)
and price fluctuations, (iii) diversify our customer base, and (iv)    使我們的客戶群多樣化，以及 (iv) 為我們的
secure a stable and reliable supply of raw materials for our refined   精細化工產品獲取穩定及可靠的原材料供
chemical products.                                                     應。

We have established several highly automation and information          我們建立了數個高度自動化和信息化系統，
systems, such as our Manufacturing Execution System (MES)              例如涵蓋我們所有生產園區的製造執行系統
that covers all of our production bases. We use the Enterprise         (MES)。我們利用企業資源計劃系統 (ERP)
Resource Planning (ERP) system and the Office Automation (OA)          和辦公自動化系統 (OA) 來管理我們的生產
system for the management of our production plan, procurement,         計劃、採購、生產、銷售、運輸、財務及業
manufacturing, sales, transportation, finance and business             務審批。有關系統積累的生產和營運數據
approvals. The production and operational data accumulated in such     可為我們的管理層提供決策依據。
systems can provide decision-making basis for our management.

management services agreement because the participants in the           第四份營運管理服務協議，原因為該行業
industry seriously take into account of our competitive strengths       的參與者認真考慮我們的競爭優勢，並願意

to the growth and development of our Group.

DIVIDEND                                                                股息
In view of the satisfactory operating results in 2018, the Board        鑑於 2018 年經營業績令人滿意，董事會建
recommends a final dividend of total amount of RMB626.6 million         議派發總金額為人民幣 626.6 百萬元的 2018
for the year of 2018 as a return to the supports of our shareholders.   年末期股息作為股東支持的回報。

OVERVIEW                                                                 概覽
We are an integrated coke, coking chemical and refined chemical          我們為中國的焦炭、焦化產品和精細化工
producer and supplier in China. The Group was the world’s largest        產品綜合生產商及供應商。根據獨立全球
independent producer and supplier of coke by volume in 2018,             諮詢公司弗若斯特沙利文（北京）諮詢有限
according to Frost & Sullivan (Beijing) Inc., Shanghai Branch Co.,       公司上海分公司（「弗若斯特沙利文」），按
(“Frost & Sullivan”) an independent global consulting firm. We also      2018 年數量計算，本集團為全球最大的獨
held leading positions in a number of refined chemical sectors in        立焦炭生產商及供應商。我們亦於中國或
China or globally. According to Frost & Sullivan, the Group was the      全球的多個精細化工產品領域處於領先地
largest producer of industrial-naphthalene-based phthalic anhydride      位。根據弗若斯特沙利文，按 2018 年數量
and coke-oven-gas-based methanol by volume in China in 2018.             計算，本集團是中國最大的工業萘製苯酐
The Group was also the largest coking crude benzene processor            及焦爐煤氣製甲醇生產商。按 2018 年數量
and the fifth largest coal tar processor by volume globally in 2018.     計算，本集團亦是全球最大的焦化粗苯加
                                                                         工商及第五大高溫煤焦油加工商。

our future prospects and other factors that our Directors consider     素而定。我們預期在其後每個年度分派不
important. We expect to distribute no less than 30% of our annual      少於年度可供分派盈利的 30% 作為股息。
distributable earnings in every subsequent year as dividends.

CHAIRMAN AND CHIEF EXECUTIVE OFFICER                                     主席及行政總裁
Mr. Yang is our chairman and chief executive officer. In accordance      楊先生為本公司主席兼行政總裁。根據上市
with Rule A.2.1 of the Corporate Governance Code as provided             規則附錄 14 企業管治守則第 A.2.1 條，主席
in Appendix 14 to the Listing Rules, the roles of the chairman           及行政總裁的職務應有區分，且不應由同一
and chief executive officer should be separated and should not           人兼任。董事會已注意到有偏離企業管治守
be held by the same person. The Board of Directors has noticed           則條文第 A.2.1 條的情況。然而，楊先生擁
that there was deviation from Rule A.2.1 of the provisions of            有豐富的焦炭、焦化及精細化工行業經驗。
the Corporate Governance Code. However, with extensive                   楊先生負責本集團的整體管理及業務發展、
experience in the coke, coking chemicals and refined chemicals           附屬公司及其相應生產設施的營運及人力
industries, Mr. Yang is responsible for the overall management           資源，自本公司於 1995 年成立以來對我們
and business development, the operations of our subsidiaries and         的業務發展及擴展有著舉足輕重的作用。
their corresponding production facilities and human resources of         董事會認為由同一人擔任主席及行政總裁
the Group and has been instrumental to our growth and business           有利於本集團的管理。高級管理層及董事
expansion since our establishment in 1995. The Board considers           會由具備豐富經驗及才能的人士組成，可
that vesting the roles of chairman and chief executive officer in the    確保權力及權限的平衡。董事會現由六名
same person is beneficial to the management of the Group. The            執行董事（包括楊先生）及三名獨立非執行
balance of power and authority is ensured by the operation of the        董事組成，因此其組成具備較強的獨立性。
senior management and the Board, which comprises experienced
and high caliber individuals. The Board currently comprises six
Executive Directors (including Mr. Yang) and three Independent
Non-executive Directors and therefore has a strong independence
element in its composition.

system with the board of directors as the core, actively responding      推動社會責任融入企業經營的方方面面。
to and meeting the demands of the stakeholders etc.

environment, employees, and investment, and direct, decide, and          部作為總牽頭及協調部門，負責日常 ESG

Example
                     “GREEN PLANT” PRACTICE OF HEBEI RISUN COKING
  案例                 河北旭陽焦化「綠色工廠」實踐




Hebei Risun Coking, actively responding to the national, provincial, and municipal calls, has been making efforts in such fields
as energy saving, environmental protection, circular economy, and low carbon with green environmental protection as the
guide, and has been improving the environmental quality and ensuring environmental safety to creating a green ecological
enterprise with blue sky, green land, clear water, and fresh air, making it one of the First Green Plants in 2017.

With respect to environmental protection, Hebei Risun Coking adhered to the general thought of “Up-to-standard Pollutant
Discharge, Effective Amount Control, Controlling Environmental Risks, Constructing Compliant Projects, Clean Production
Process, and Recycling Resources”, focusing on advanced treatment of pollution source and improving essential up-to-
standard discharge and emphasizing on the optimization and improvement of environmental protection facilities under the
support of technology, process, and management innovation, and through multiple environmental protection reconstruction
projects, such as enclosure of the coal yard, advanced treatment of coking wastewater, and dry quenching, by replacing the
high-efficiency dust remover in the whole plant, to promote cleaner production in all sectors.

With respect to resources utilization, Hebei Risun Coking fully implemented the energy management modular construction
with the energy management center as the platform and taking the requirements of quality, environmental protection, and
safety under the premises of compliance with national and governmental laws and regulations and standards, incorporating
lean management into the energy management system and promoting the implementation of the energy diagnosis project
under the guideline of energy system diagnosis and energy saving plan. Hebei Risun Coking will emphasize on the dry
quenching project and study and promote the implementation of the ascending pipe waste head utilization project to improve
the utilization efficiency of such energy as waste heat and waste energy, coal gas, steam, electricity and water, maximizing
the energy value.

With respect to recycling economy, Hebei Risun Coking kept improving the processes and technologies and carrying
out resources saving work to further reduce the main resource consumption indicators, increase the resource utilization
efficiency, and reduce wastes discharge based on the idea of green, low-carbon, and recycling development.

為積極響應國家和省、市號召，多年以來河北旭陽焦化以綠色環保為先導，在節能、環保、循環經濟、低碳等領域繼續投
入力量，不斷改善環境質量、確保環境安全，創建天藍、地綠、水清、氣淨的綠色生態企業，2017 年被評為首批綠色工
廠。

在環境保護方面，河北旭陽焦化繼續秉持「污染排放達標、總量控制有效、環境風險受控、建設項目合規、生產過程清潔、
資源循環利用」的總體思路，進一步著眼於污染源深度治理、本質化達標提升，以技術、工藝、管理創新為支撐，突出環
保設施優化完善，全廠通過替換高效除塵器，實施煤場全封閉、焦化廢水深度處理、乾熄焦等多個環保改造項目，全面推
行清潔生產。

在能源利用方面，以能源管理中心為平台，綜合考慮質量、環保、安全等方面要求，在滿足國家政府法律法規和標準要求
條件下，全面實施能源管理模塊化建設，將精益管理融入能源管理體系，以能源系統診斷、節能規劃為綱，推進實施能源
診斷項目，下一步將重點實施乾熄焦項目，研究推進上升管余熱利用項目實施等，不斷提高余熱余能、煤氣、蒸汽、電、
水等能源利用效率，實現能源價值最大化。

在循環經濟方面，按照綠色、低碳、循環發展理念，持續進行工藝技術優化和開展資源節約工作，進一步降低煉焦煤消耗
和水資源消耗等主要資源消耗指標，提高資源利用效率和減少廢棄物的排放。

     Example
                       TANGSHAN RISUN CHEMICALS CARRIED OUT DRILLS OF HAZARDOUS
       案例              CHEMICALS LEAKAGE EMERGENCIES TO IMPROVE THE ABILITY TO
                       HANDLE ENVIRONMENT EMERGENCIES
                       唐山旭陽化工開展危化品洩漏應急演練，提高突發環境事件應急處置能力




                                           In June, 2018, Tangshan Risun Chemicals for further improving the
    TO IMPROVE THE                         ability to prevent environment emergencies, organized and carried
    ABILITY TO HANDLE                      out the drills of environment emergencies to simulate environment
    ENVIRONMENT                            emergencies caused by fire arising from leakage of benzene tank:
                                           Upon the occurrence of leakage, notified the employees to evacuate
    EMERGENCIES                            immediately and cut off the drain value of clean sewage timely;
                                           the rescue crew put on protective clothes and oxygen respirator
    提高突發環境事件                               and entered the site for leaking stoppage and handling, put out the
    應急處置能力                                 fire timely and started the valve of the pool in accident finally and
                                           delivered the leakage collected to the pool in accident to prevent
                                           the impact of liquid leakage on the environment. The emergency
                                           drill improved the ability to handle environment emergencies, which
                                           ensures the environmental safety of the surroundings.

                                           為進一步提高突發環境事件應急防範能力，2018 年 6 月唐山旭陽化
                                           工組織開展突發環境事件應急演練，模擬苯罐洩漏引發的火災造成
                                           突發環境事件情景：洩漏發生後，緊急通知人員撤離疏散，及時切
                                           斷公司清淨下水的排放閥，搶險救災組穿戴好防護服、氧氣呼吸器
                                           進入現場堵漏處理，及時撲滅火災，最後啓動事故水池閥門，將收
                                           集的洩漏液打至事故池，防止液體外溢對環境造成的影響。此次應
                                           急演練提高了對突發環境事件應急處置能力，確保維護周邊地區環
                                           境安全。

  Example            XINGTAI RISUN CHEMICALS CO., LTD. CARRIED OUT EMERGENCY RESCUE DRILL
    案例               邢台旭陽化工開展事故應急救援演練

  Xingtai Risun Chemicals taking the opportunity of the safety month of
  the year, actively carried out various safety activities, including organizing
  employees to learn various accident cases, taking part in the competition
  of safety skills of Xingtai Production Base and the emergency rescue
  drill. Xingtai Risun Chemicals also specially organized the emergency
  rescue drill of carbon monoxide poisoning. The whole process of drill
  was closely connected and carried out orderly. Upon completion of the
  drill, Xingtai Risun Chemicals emphasized the importance of emergency
  rescue drill and required all employees participating the drill to make a
  summary carefully and learn useful theoretical knowledge about safety
                                   “SAFE PLANTS”
  from relevant accident cases, so as to get necessary safety skills and
  provide strong support for building “Safe Plants”.

  邢台旭陽化工以今年安全月為契機，積極開展各項安全活動，包括組織
                                  「安全工廠」
  人員學習各種事故案例、參加邢台生產園區安全技能比武和應急救援演
  練等。此外，還專門組織了針對煤氣中毒事故的應急救援演練。整個演
  練過程銜接緊密，有條不紊。演練結束後，重點強調了應急救援演練的
  重要性，並要求全體參演員工將演練進行認真總結，同時多學習有關的
  事故案例，從中汲取有用的安全理論知識，不斷充實必備的安全技能，
  從而為打造「安全工廠」提供有力保障。

OCCUPATIONAL HEALTH AND TRAINING                                        職業健康與培訓
The Group, adhering to the guideline of “focusing on prevention         本集團始終堅持「預防為主、防治結合」的
and combining prevention with control” and stringently complying        方針，嚴格遵守《中華人民共和國安全生產
with relevant laws and regulations such as the Production Safety        法》
                                                                         《中華人民共和國職業病防治法》等相關

Republic of China on Prevention and Control of Occupational             化職業病危害防治措施，定期組織員工體
Diseases, strengthens the occupational health and safety                檢，確保員工職業健康。同時，我們持續完

Occupational Health Management                                          職業健康管理
The Group evaluates the hazard factors of occupational diseases         本集團動態評估作業場所職業病危害因素，
in the workplace dynamically, organizes the identification and          組織開展覆蓋所有作業場所的職業病危害
detection of occupational disease hazard factors covering all           因素識別與檢測工作，職業危害強度或濃度
workplaces, finding out the occupational hazard intensity or            符合國家標準、行業標準，並將檢測結果向
concentration meeting the national standards and industrial             員工告知；同時根據焦化行業特點和企業職
standards, and informs the employees of the results; strengthens        業病的發病類型，有針對性地加強對重點
the monitoring of key steps, key posts, and key groups specifically,    環節、重點崗位、重點人群的監測監控，開
carries out special treatment of occupational hazards of key            展重點崗位職業危害專項治理，實施好源
posts, implements source control and process management,                頭控制和過程管理，加強健康監護，發現可
strengthen health monitoring, and shift the work and post timely        疑症狀，及時調換工作崗位，減少職業病的
in case of any suspicious symptoms to reduce the incidence              發生；在職工個體防護方面，針對不同崗位
of occupational diseases according to the characteristics of the        涉及的職業病危害因素不同，按要求配備
coking industry and the types of occupational diseases of the           勞保用品，並進行定期維護或更換，確保正
Group; with respect to personal protection of employees, the            常使用；對從事接觸職業病危害的作業的
Group provides labor protective supplies and carries out periodic       勞動者，本集團嚴格按照國務院安全生產
maintenance or replacement to ensure their normal use based on          監督管理部門、衛生行政部門的規定組織
different occupational disease hazard factors of different posts; for   上崗前、在崗期間和離崗時的職業健康檢
workers engaged in operations exposed to occupational disease           查，並將檢查結果書面告知員工。
hazards, the Group organizes pre-work, on-the-post, and off-post
occupational health examinations stringently in accordance with the
provisions of the safety production supervision and management
department and the health administrative department of the State
Council and inform the employees of the results in writing.

hours a week and sets up the overtime and holiday system to            避免強迫或強制勞動，保證員工合理工時。
prevent forced or compulsory labor and ensure reasonable working

   Example          XINGTAI PRODUCTION BASE HELD THE CADRES TRAINING CLASS JOINTLY
     案例             WITH XINGTAI POLYTECHNIC COLLEGE
                    邢台生產園區與邢台職業技術學院聯合舉辦幹部培訓班




         For promoting the construction of the cadres team of Xingtai Production
         Base, effectively improving the management ability of assistant cadres,
         and motivating the learning enthusiasm, Xingtai Production Base held
         the assistant cadres training class jointly with Xingtai Polytechnic
         College. The training class adopted the training form integrated with
         theory teaching, interactive training, and sand table simulation to make
         assistant cadres to keep in mind the “Two Hows”, fully realize the
         importance of the study and put them into action, proposing good
         suggestions on management of the department and playing the role of
         mainstay in practice, being able to undertake tasks alone and making
         new contributions to the development of the Group.

         為促進邢台生產園區幹部梯隊建設，有效提升助理級幹部管理能力，激
         發學習活力，邢台生產園區聯合邢台職業技術學院合作開辦了助理級幹
         部培訓班。培訓班採用理論講授，互動培訓，沙盤模擬於一體的培訓形
         式，旨在使助理級幹部牢記旭陽「兩個如何」，充分認識到本次學習的重
         要性，並且做到學以致用，對部門管理工作提出好的建議意見，切實在
         實際工作中發揮中流砥柱的作用，獨當一面，為本集團發展做出新的貢
         獻。

The Group attaches great importance to green supply chain               本集團注重綠色供應鏈管理，採取審慎的

ability and enterprise reputation, but also the philosophy and          勞工標準等履行社會責任的理念和行動。

the QHSE standards and labor standards. We adheres to the               全面實施集中統一、分類分級供貨商管理，
philosophy of “Win-Win Cooperation and Mutual Development”,             加強培訓和監督，優化績效考核，實現供應

                        XINGTAI PRODUCTION BASE ASSISTED THE
      Example
                        CONSTRUCTION OF LOCAL COMMUNITY
        案例
                        邢台生產園區助力當地社區建設

An enterprise cannot survive and develop without the support of
good relationship with the mass. In recent years, Xingtai Production
Base of the Group adhered to the win-win cooperation with private
enterprises and returned to the locals through the government,
extending the development dividend to the people around and of the
counties of Xingtai City. Xingtai Production Base actively took part in
local special and economic construction from the “719” flood-fighting
and emergency rescues to helping and supporting the poor, and from
road hardening and landscaping to village resettlement. On the eve of
the Chinese New Year of 2018, Xingtai Production Base, cared for the
poor people such as the old party members, military vet, “NINA” and
people with serious disease of nearby villages, and organized a visit to
them and sent rice, oil and other necessities to them.

企業的生存發展離不開良好群眾關係的支撐。近年來，本集團邢台生
產園區堅持民企共贏，依託政府回饋於民，將發展的紅利惠及到周邊
乃至邢台縣市人民，從「719」抗洪搶險到幫扶救困，從道路硬化、環
境美化到村莊搬遷，邢台生產園區積極參
與當地社會經濟建設。2018 年 春節前夕，
邢台生產園區惦念周邊村莊老黨員、退役
老兵、「雙無」、重病等家庭生活困難群眾，
特組織走訪慰問活動為他們送去米、油等
生活必需品。

AUDITOR’S RESPONSIBILITIES FOR THE AUDIT OF                               核數師就審計綜合財務報表承擔的
THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS                                     責任
Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether           吾等的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否
the consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from            不存在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤
material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue        陳述取得合理保證，並出具包括吾等意見

     below.

     Shareholders obtained its control on Cangzhou Risun on                 東於 2016 年 6 月 8 日修訂滄州旭陽細

     INTERNATIONAL FINANCIAL REPORTING                                          告準則（「國際財務報告準則」
                                                                                             ）
     STANDARDS (“IFRSs”) (Continued)                                            （續）

     NEW AND REVISED IFRSs IN ISSUE BUT NOT YET                                 已頒佈但尚未生效的新訂及經修
     EFFECTIVE (Continued)                                                      訂國際財務報告準則（續）
     IFRS 16 Leases                                                             國際財務報告準則第 16 號租賃
     IFRS 16 introduces a comprehensive model for the                           國際財務報告準則第 16 號為識別出租
     identification of lease arrangements and accounting                        人及承租人的租賃安排及會計處理引
     treatments for both lessors and lessees. IFRS 16 will                      入一個綜合模式。國際財務報告準則
     supersede IAS 17 “Leases” and the related interpretations                  第 16 號於生效時將取代國際會計準則
     when it becomes effective.                                                 第 17 號「租賃」及相關詮釋。

     IFRS 16 distinguishes lease and service contracts on the                   國際財務報告準則第 16 號根據所識別
     basis of whether an identified asset is controlled by a                    資產是否由客戶控制區分租賃及服務
     customer. Distinctions of operating leases (off balance sheet)             合約。經營租賃（資產負債表以外）及
     and finance leases (on balance sheet) are removed for lessee               融資租賃（資產負債表以內）的差異自
     accounting, and is replaced by a model where a right-of-use                承租人會計處理中撤銷，並由承租人
     asset and a corresponding liability have to be recognized for              須就所有租賃確認使用權資產及相應
     all leases by lessees, except for short-term leases and leases             負債的模式替代，惟短期租賃及低價
     of low value assets.                                                       值資產租賃則除外。

     The right-of-use asset is initially measured at cost and                   使用權資產初步按成本計量，其後按
     subsequently measured at cost (subject to certain                          成本（有若干例外情況）減累計折舊及
     exceptions) less accumulated depreciation and impairment                   減值虧損計量，並就租賃負債的任何
     losses, adjusted for any remeasurement of the lease                        重新計量作出調整。租賃負債初步按
     liability. The lease liability is initially measured at the present        未付租賃款項當日有關租賃款項的現
     value of the lease payments that are not paid at that date.                值 計 量。其 後，租 賃 負 債 就（其 中 包
     Subsequently, the lease liability is adjusted for interest and             括）利息、租賃款項及租約修訂的影響
     lease payments, as well as the impact of lease modifications,              作出調整。就現金流量分類而言，本
     amongst others. For the classification of cash flows, the                  集團現時將預付租賃款項呈列為與自
     Group currently presents upfront prepaid lease payments as                 用租賃土地有關的投資現金流量，而
     investing cash flows in relation to leasehold lands for owned              其他經營租賃款項則呈列為經營現金
     use while other operating lease payments are presented as                  流量。於應用國際財務報告準則第 16
     operating cash flows.. Upon application of IFRS 16, lease                  號後，有關租賃負債的租賃款項將分
     payments in relation to lease liability will be allocated into             為本金及利息部分，並會呈列為本集
     a principal and an interest portion which will be presented                團的融資現金流量，預付租賃款項將
     as financing cash flows by the Group, upfront prepaid                      按適用情況繼續根據性質呈列為投資
     lease payments will continue to be presented as investing                  或經營現金流量。
     or operating cash flows in accordance to the nature, as
     appropriate.

4.   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                      4.   主要會計政策
     The consolidated financial statements has been prepared                   綜合財務報表乃根據國際會計準則理
     in accordance with IFRSs issued by IASB. In addition,                     事會頒佈的國際財務報告準則編製。
     the consolidated financial statements includes applicable                 此外，綜合財務報表包括聯交所證券
     disclosures required by the Rules Governing the Listing of                上市規則及香港公司條例規定的適用
     Securities on the Stock Exchange and by the Hong Kong                     披露。
     Companies Ordinance.

     The consolidated financial statements has been prepared                   綜合財務報表乃按歷史成本基準編
     on the historical cost basis except for certain financial                 製，惟若干金融工具如下文載列的會
     instruments that are measured at fair values at the end of                計政策所述於各報告期末以公允值計
     each reporting period, as explained in the accounting policies            量除外。
     below.

     Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the               歷史成本通常基於換取商品及服務交
     consideration given in exchange for goods and services.                   易所付出代價的公允值。

     Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset           公允值是於計量日期由市場參與者於
     or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between         有秩序交易中出售資產可收取或轉讓
     market participants at the measurement date, regardless of                負債須支付的價格，而不論該價格是
     whether that price is directly observable or estimated using              否直接可觀察或可使用其他估值技術
     another valuation technique. In estimating the fair value                 估計。若市場參與者於計量日期對資
     of an asset or a liability, the Group takes into account the              產或負債定價時會考慮資產或負債的
     characteristics of the asset or liability if market participants          特點，則本集團於估計資產或負債的
     would take those characteristics into account when pricing                公允值時會考慮該等特點。綜合財務
     the asset or liability at the measurement date. Fair value for            報表中作計量及╱或披露用途的公允
     measurement and/or disclosure purposes in the consolidated                值乃按此基準釐定，惟屬於國際會計
     financial statements is determined on such a basis, except                準 則 第 17 號「租 賃」範 圍 內 的 租 賃 交
     for leasing transactions that are within the scope of IAS 17              易，以及與公允值有部份相若地方但
     “Leases”, and measurements that have some similarities to                 並非公允值的計量，譬如國際會計準
     fair value but are not fair value, such as net realizable value in        則第 2 號「存貨」內的可變現淨值或國
     IAS 2 “Inventories” or value in use in IAS 36 “Impairment of              際會計準則第 36 號「資產減值」的使用
     Assets”.                                                                  價值除外。

     A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into              非金融資產的公允值計量須計及一名
     account a market participant’s ability to generate economic               市場參與者透過使用其資產的最高及
     benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by             最佳用途或透過將資產出售予將使用
     selling it to another market participant that would use the               其最高及最佳用途的另一名市場參與
     asset in its highest and best use.                                        者而能夠產生經濟利益的能力。

     For financial instruments which are transferred at fair value             按公允值轉讓的金融工具，凡於其後
     and a valuation technique that unobservable inputs is to                  期間應用以不可觀察輸入數據計量公
     be used to measure fair value in subsequent periods, the                  允值的估值方法，估值方法應予校正，
     valuation technique is calibrated so that the results of the              以使估值方法的結果相等於交易價格。
     valuation technique equals the transaction price.

     In addition, for financial reporting purposes, fair value               此外，就財務申報而言，公允值計量根
     measurements are categorized into Level 1, 2 or 3                       據公允值計量的輸入數據可觀察程度
     based on the degree to which the inputs to the fair value               及輸入數據對公允值計量整體的重要
     measurements are observable and the significance of the                 性分類為第一級、第二級或第三級，
     inputs to the fair value measurement in its entirety, which are         其載述如下：
     described as follows:

     •    Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active            •   第一級輸入數據是實體於計量日
          markets for identical assets or liabilities that the entity            期可以取得的相同資產或負債於
          can access at the measurement date;                                    活躍市場的報價（未經調整）；

     •    Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted prices                •    第二級輸入數據是就資產或負債
          included within Level 1, that are observable for the                    直接或間接地可觀察的輸入數據
          asset or liability, either directly or indirectly; and                 （第一級內包括的報價除外）；及

     •    Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset or            •   第三級輸入數據是資產或負債的
          liability.                                                             不可觀察輸入數據。

     The principal accounting policies adopted are set out below.            所採納的主要會計政策載於下文。

     BASIS OF CONSOLIDATION                                                  綜合基準
     The consolidated financial statements incorporates the                  綜合財務報表包含本公司及本公司控
     financial statements of the Company and entities controlled             制的實體及其附屬公司的財務報表。
     by the Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved                本公司於以下情況擁有控制權：
     where the Company:

     •    has power over the investee;                                       •   可對投資對象行使權力；

     •    is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its            •   因參與投資對象之營運而承受所
          involvement with the investee; and                                     得浮動回報之風險或享有回報之
                                                                                 權利；及

     •    has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.            •   能行使權力影響回報。

     The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee             倘有事實及情況顯示上述三個控制因
     if facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to           素其中一個或以上有變，本集團會重
     one or more of the three elements of control listed above.              新評估其是否控制投資對象。

     Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group obtains             本集團在取得附屬公司的控制權時開
     control over the subsidiary and ceases when the Group loses             始將該附屬公司綜合入賬，並於失去
     control of the subsidiary. Specifically, income and expenses            該附屬公司的控制權時終止綜合入
     of a subsidiary acquired or disposed of during the year are             賬。具體而言，於年內所收購或出售
     included in the consolidated statements of profit or loss and           的附屬公司的收入及開支由本集團取
     other comprehensive income from the date the Group gains                得控制權當日開始計入綜合損益及其
     control until the date when the Group ceases to control the             他全面收益表，直至本集團不再控制
     subsidiary.                                                             該附屬公司當日為止。

     VENTURES
     An associate is an entity over which the Group has significant          聯營公司是指本集團有重大影響力的
     influence. Significant influence is the power to participate in         實體。重大影響力指有權參與被投資
     the financial and operating policy decisions of the investee but        方的財務及經營政策決定但非控制或
     is not control or joint control over those policies.                    共同控制該等政策。

     A joint venture is a joint arrangement whereby the parties              合營企業為一種合營安排，據此，對
     that have joint control of the arrangement have rights to               安排擁有共同控制權的訂約方對合營
     the net assets of the joint arrangement. Joint control is the           安排的淨資產享有權利。共同控制權
     contractually agreed sharing of control of an arrangement,              指按合約協定共享對安排的控制權，
     which exists only when decisions about the relevant activities          僅於與相關活動有關的決策須獲共享
     require unanimous consent of the parties sharing control.               控制權的訂約方一致同意時存在。

     The results and assets and liabilities of associates or joint            聯營公司或合營企業的業績與資產及

     statements using the equity method of accounting. The                    務報表。聯營公司及合營企業用於權
     financial statements of associates and joint ventures used               益會計處理的財務報表按與本集團於
     for equity accounting purposes are prepared using uniform                類似情況就同類交易及事件所遵循者
     accounting policies as those of the Group for like transactions          一致的會計政策編製。根據權益法，於
     and events in similar circumstances. Under the equity                    一間聯營公司或一間合營企業的投資
     method, an investment in an associate or a joint venture is              初步按成本於綜合財務狀況表確認，
     initially recognized in the consolidated statement of financial          並於其後就確認本集團應佔該聯營公
     position at cost and adjusted thereafter to recognize the                司或合營企業的損益及其他全面收益
     Group’s share of the profit or loss and other comprehensive              作出調整。聯營公司╱合營企業淨資
     income of the associate or joint venture. Changes in net                 產（損益及其他全面收益除外）的變動

     loss and other comprehensive income are not accounted                    變，否則不予入賬。當本集團應佔一

     Any excess of the Group’s share of the net fair value of                公允淨值超出投資成本的任何差額，

     REVENUE RECOGNITION                                                     收益確認
     Revenue is recognized to depict the transfer of a product               確認以描述向客戶轉移產品或服務的
     or service to a customer in an amount that reflects the                 收益金額，反映實體預期就交換該等
     consideration to which the entity expects to be entitled in             產品或服務有權獲得的代價。具體而
     exchange for those products or services. Specially, the Group           言，本集團使用確認收益的 5 個步驟：
     uses a 5-step approach to revenue recognition:

     Step 1: Identify the contract(s) with a customer                        步驟 1： 識別與客戶訂立的合約

     Step 2: Identify the performance obligations in the contract            步驟 2： 識別合約內的履約責任

     Step 3: Determine the transaction price                                 步驟 3： 釐定交易價

     Step 4: Allocate the transaction price to the performance               步驟 4： 將交易價分配至合約內的履
             obligations in the contract                                           約責任

     Step 5: Recognize revenue when (or as) the entity satisfies a           步驟 5： 於實體完成履約責任時（或就
             performance obligation                                                此）確認收益

     The Group recognizes revenue when (or as) a performance                 本集團於完成履約責任時（或就此）確
     obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the products or         認收益，即於特定履約責任相關的產
     services underlying the particular performance obligation is            品或服務的「控制權」轉讓予客戶時。
     transferred to the customers.

     A performance obligation represents a good or service (or               履約責任指一個明確貨品或服務（或
     a bundle or goods or services) that is distinct or a series of          一批明確貨品或服務）或一系列大致
     distinct goods or services that are substantially the same.             相同的明確貨品或服務。

     Principal versus agent                                                    委託人與代理人
     When another party is involved in providing goods or services              當另一方從事向客戶提供貨品或服
     to a customer, the Group determines whether the nature                     務，本集團釐定其承諾之性質是否為
     of its promise is a performance obligation to provide the                  提供指定貨品或服務本身之履約責任
     specified goods or services itself (i.e. the Group is a principal)        （即本集團為委託人）或安排由另一方
     or to arrange for those goods or services to be provided by the            提供該等貨品或服務（即本集團為代
     other party (i.e. the Group is an agent).                                  理人）。

     The Group is a principal if it is controls the specified good             倘本集團在向客戶轉讓貨品或服務之
     or service before that good or service is transferred to a                前控制指定貨品或服務，則本集團為
     customer.                                                                 委託人。

     The Group is an agent if its performance obligation is to                 倘本集團之履約責任為安排另一方提
     arrange for the provision of the specified good or service by             供指定的貨品或服務，則本集團為代
     another party. In this case, the Group does not control the               理人。在此情况下，在將貨品或服務
     specified good or service provided by another party before                轉讓予客戶之前，本集團不控制另一
     that good or service is transferred to the customer. When the             方提供的指定貨品或服務。當本集團
     Group acts as an agent, it recognizes revenue in the amount               為代理人時，應就為換取另一方安排
     of any fee or commission to which it expects to be entitled in            提供的指定貨品或服務預期有權取得
     exchange for arranging for the specified goods or services to             之任何費用或佣金之金額確認收益。
     be provided by the other party.

     LEASING                                                                   租賃
     Leases are classified as finance leases whenever the terms                當租賃條款已將所有權絕大部分風險
     of the lease transfer substantially all the risks and rewards             及回報轉移至承租人時，租賃分類為
     of ownership to the lessee. All other leases are classified as            融資租賃。其他所有租賃分類為經營
     operating leases.                                                         租賃。

     The Group as lessor                                                       本集團作為出租人
     Rental income from operating lease is recognized in profit                經營租賃的租金收入於相關租約租期
     or loss on a straight-line basis over the term of the relevant            內以直線法於損益中確認。磋商及安
     lease. Initial direct costs incurred in negotiating and arranging         排經營租約產生的初始直接成本，計
     an operating lease are added to the carrying amount of the                入出租資產的賬面值，並以直線法在
     leased asset and recognized as an expense on a straight-line              租賃年期確認為開支。
     basis over the lease term.

     BORROWING COSTS                                                          借款成本
     Borrowing costs directly attributable to the acquisition of              直接歸屬於收購合資格資產（指必須
     qualifying assets, which are assets that necessarily take a              經一段長時間處理以作其預定用途或
     substantial period of time to get ready for their intended use           銷售的資產）的借款成本，加入該等資
     or sale, are added to the cost of those assets, until such time          產的成本內，直至資產大致上備妥供
     as the assets are substantially ready for their intended use or          其預定用途或銷售為止。
     sale.

     All other borrowing costs are recognized in profit or loss in the        所有其他借款成本於產生期內的損益
     period in which they are incurred.                                       中確認。

     GOVERNMENT GRANTS                                                        政府補助
     Government grants are not recognized until there is a                    政府補助在可合理保證本集團將遵守
     reasonable assurance that the Group will comply with the                 補助所附帶條件且將接獲補助時方予
     conditions attaching to them and that the grants will be                 確認。
     received.

     Government grants are recognized in profit or loss on                     政府補助乃就本集團確認的有關開支
     a systematic basis over the periods in which the Group                   （預期補助可予抵銷成本的開支）期間
     recognizes as expenses the related costs for which the                    按系統化的基準於損益中確認。具體
     grants are intended to compensate. Specifically, government               而言，以要求本集團購買、建造或收購
     grants whose primary condition is that the Group should                   非流動資產為主要條件的政府補助乃
     purchase, construct or otherwise acquire non-current assets               於綜合財務狀況表確認為遞延收入，
     are recognized as deferred income in the consolidated                     並於相關資產的可用年期內基於系統
     statement of financial position and transferred to profit or loss         合理基準轉撥至損益中。
     on a systematic and rational basis over the useful lives of the
     related assets.

     Government grants that are receivable as compensation for                作為補償已產生開支或虧損的應收款
     expenses or losses already incurred or for the purpose of                項或為本集團提供即時財務資助（不
     giving immediate financial support to the Group with no future           涉及未來相關成本）的政府補助於成
     related costs are recognized in profit or loss in the period in          為應收款項期間於損益內確認。
     which they become receivable.

     RETIREMENT BENEFIT SCHEME CONTRIBUTION                                   退休福利計劃供款

     bureau in accordance with the government regulations                     （「中國」）政府規例應支付予由地方社
     in Hong Kong and of the People’s Republic of China (the                   會保障局管理退休福利計劃的供款於
     “PRC”), are recognized as an expense when employees have                  僱員已提供使其有權享有該等供款的
     rendered service entitling them to the contributions.                     服務時作為開支確認。

     TAXATION                                                                  稅項
     Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently                所得稅開支指現時應付的稅項及遞延
     payable and deferred tax. The tax currently payable is based              稅項總額。現時應付的稅項乃以年內
     on taxable profit for the year. Taxable profit differs from profit        的應課稅溢利為基準。應課稅溢利與
     before taxation as reported in the consolidated statement                 綜合損益及其他全面收益表中呈報的
     of profit or loss and other comprehensive income because                  除稅前溢利有別，因其不包括在於其
     it excludes items of income or expense that are taxable or                他年度應課稅或可扣除的收入或開支
     deductible in other years and items that are never taxable or             項目以及永不課稅或不可扣除的項
     deductible. The Group’s liability for current tax is calculated           目。本集團有關即期稅項的負債按各
     using tax rates that have been enacted or substantively                   報告期末前已頒佈或大致上已頒佈的
     enacted by the end of each reporting period.                              稅率計算。

     Deferred tax is recognized on temporary differences                       遞延稅項指就按照於綜合財務報表呈
     between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the             列資產及負債賬面值與根據用作計算
     consolidated financial statements and the corresponding tax               應課稅溢利的相關稅基間所確認的暫
     base used in the computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax              時差異。遞延稅項負債一般就所有應
     liabilities are generally recognized for all taxable temporary            課稅暫時差異確認。遞延稅項資產則
     differences. Deferred tax assets are generally recognized                 一般在應課稅溢利可能將用作抵銷可
     for all deductible temporary differences to the extent that is            動用的應扣除暫時差異時確認所有應
     probable that taxable profits will be available against which             扣除暫時差異。倘因初步確認交易中
     those deductible temporary differences can be utilized. Such              資產與負債（業務合併除外）而引起暫
     deferred tax assets and liabilities are not recognized if the             時差異或對應課稅溢利或會計溢利均
     temporary difference arises from the initial recognition (other           無影響時，將不會確認該等遞延稅項
     than in a business combination) of assets and liabilities in              資產與負債。此外，倘暫時差異產生
     a transaction that affects neither the taxable profit nor the             自商譽初步確認，則不會確認遞延稅
     accounting profit. In addition, deferred tax liabilities are not          項負債。
     recognized if the temporary difference arises from the initial
     recognition of goodwill.

     Deferred tax liabilities are recognized for taxable temporary            遞延稅項負債乃按與於附屬公司的投
     difference associated with investments in subsidiaries and               資及於聯營公司和合營企業的權益相
     interests in associates and joint ventures, except where                 關的應課稅暫時差異而確認，惟本集
     the Group is able to control the reversal of the temporary               團有能力控制暫時差異的轉撥及暫時
     difference and it is probable that the temporary difference              差異將可能不會於可見將來轉撥除
     will not reverse in the foreseeable future. Deferred tax assets          外，而因與該等投資及權益有關的應
     arising from deductible temporary differences associated                 扣除暫時差異而產生的遞延稅項資
     with such investments and interests are only recognized                  產，僅在可能有足夠應課稅溢利以動
     to the extent that it is probable that there will be sufficient          用暫時差異利益及預期於可見將來轉
     taxable profits against which to utilize the benefits of the             撥時確認。
     temporary differences and they are expected to reverse in the
     foreseeable future.

     The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at the            遞延稅項資產賬面值乃於各報告期末
     end of each reporting period and reduced to the extent that              審閱，並減至再無足夠應課稅溢利以
     it is no longer probable that sufficient taxable profits will be         收回全部或部份資產。
     available to allow all or part of the asset to be recovered.

     Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax              遞延稅項資產及負債乃以預期於償付
     rates that are expected to apply in the period in which the              負債或變現資產的期間應用的稅率，
     liability is settled or the asset is realized, based on tax rates        根據於各報告期末已頒佈或實質上已
     (and tax laws) that have been enacted or substantively                   頒佈的稅率（及稅法）計算。
     enacted by the end of each reporting period.

     The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets                   遞延稅項負債及資產的計算結果反映
     reflects the tax consequences that would follow from the                 本集團於各報告期末預期就收回或償
     manner in which the Group expects, at the end of each                    付資產及負債賬面值可能引致的稅務
     reporting period, to recover or settle the carrying amounts of           後果。
     its assets and liabilities.

     Deferred tax assets and liabilities are offset when there is a           如有法定可執行權利將即期稅項資產
     legally enforceable right to set off current tax assets against          抵銷即期稅項負債，而遞延稅項資產
     current tax liabilities and when they relate to income taxes             及負債與同一稅務部門徵收的所得稅
     levied by the same taxation authority and the Group intends to           有關，且本集團有意按淨額基準結算
     settle its current tax assets and liabilities on a net basis.            其即期稅項資產及負債，則可抵銷遞
                                                                              延稅項資產及負債。

     Current and deferred tax are recognized in profit or loss,               即期及遞延稅項於損益中確認，惟與
     except when it relates to items that are recognized in other             於其他全面收益或直接於權益確認的
     comprehensive income or directly in equity, in which case,               項目相關者除外，於該情況下，即期
     the current and deferred tax are also recognized in other                及遞延稅項亦分別於其他全面收益或
     comprehensive income or directly in equity respectively.                 直接於權益確認。倘因對業務合併進
     Where current tax or deferred tax arises from the initial                行初始會計處理而產生即期稅項或遞

     included in the accounting for the business combination.                 會計處理內。

     Impairment under ECL model                                                預期信貸虧損模式項下減值
     The Group recognizes a loss allowance for ECL on financial                本集團就受限於國際財務報告準則第
     assets which are subject to impairment under IFRS 9                       9 號項下減值之金融資產（包括其他應
     (including other receivables, trade and bills receivables                 收款項、按公允值計入其他全面收益
     measured at FVTOCI, amounts due from related parties and                  計量的貿易應收款項及應收票據、應
     bank balances) and financial guarantee contracts. The amount              收關聯方款項及銀行結餘）及財務擔
     of ECL is updated at the end of each reporting period to                  保合約相關的預期信貸虧損確認虧損
     reflect changes in credit risk since initial recognition.                 撥備。預期信貸虧損金額於各報告期
                                                                               末予以更新，以反映信貸風險自初始
                                                                               確認以來的變動。

     Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from                     全期預期信貸虧損指於相關工具預期
     all possible default events over the expected life of the                 年期內發生的所有可能違約事件所導
     relevant instrument. In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m ECL”)                致的預期信貸虧損。與此相對，12 個
     represents the portion of lifetime ECL that is expected to                月預期信貸虧損（「12 個月預期信貸虧
     result from default events that are possible within 12 months             損」）指預期於各報告期末後 12 個月內
     after the end of each reporting period. Assessments are                   可能發生的違約事件所導致的部分全
     done based on the Group’s historical credit loss experience,              期預期信貸虧損。評估根據本集團過
     adjusted for factors that are specific to the debtors, general            往信貸虧損經驗進行，並根據應收賬
     economic conditions and an assessment of both the current                 款特定因素、整體經濟狀況以及於各
     conditions at the end of each reporting period as well as the             報告期末對當前狀況及未來狀況預測
     forecast of future conditions.                                            的評估而作出調整。

     The Group always recognizes lifetime ECL for trade                        本集團始終就貿易應收款項及與客戶
     receivables and amounts due from related parties arising                  簽訂合約產生的應收關聯方款項確認
     from contracts with customers. The ECL on these assets                    全期預期信貸虧損。該等資產的預期
     are assessed collectively using a provision matrix with                   信貸虧損使用具有適當分組的撥備矩
     appropriate groupings except that significant balances are                陣進行集體評估（重大結餘個別評估
     assessed individually.                                                    除外）。

     For all other instruments, the Group measures the loss                    就所有其他工具而言，本集團計量虧
     allowance equal to 12m ECL, unless when there has been a                  損 撥 備 等 於 12 個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧 損，
     significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition, the        除非當信貸風險自初始確認以來顯著
     Group recognizes lifetime ECL. The assessment of whether                  上升，則本集團確認全期預期信貸虧
     lifetime ECL should be recognized is based on significant                 損。評估是否應確認全期預期信貸虧
     increases in the likelihood of risk of a default occurring since          損乃根據自初始確認以來發生違約風
     initial recognition.                                                      險的可能性顯著上升。

     Significant increase in credit risk                                       信貸風險大幅增加
     In assessing whether the credit risk has increased                        於評估信貸風險是否自初始確認以來
     significantly since initial recognition, the Group compares the           大幅增加時，本集團比較金融工具於
     risk of a default occurring on the financial instrument as at the         各報告期末出現違約的風險與該金融
     end of each reporting period with the risk if a default occurring         工具於初始確認日期出現違約的風
     on the financial instrument as at the date of initial recognition.        險。作此評估時，本集團會考慮合理
     In making this assessment, the Group considers both                       及有理據的定量及定性資料，包括歷
     quantitative and qualitative information that is reasonable               史經驗及毋須花費不必要成本或精力
     and supportable, including historical experience and forward-             即可獲得的前瞻性資料。
     looking information that is available without undue cost or
     effort.

     In particular, the following information is taken into                    尤其是，評估信貸風險是否大幅增加
     account when assessing whether credit risk has increased                  時會考慮下列資料：
     significantly:

     •     an actual or expected significant deterioration in the              •     金融工具外部（如有）或內部信
           financial instrument’s external (if available) or internal                貸評級的實際或預期重大惡化；
           credit rating;

     •     significant deterioration in external market indicators             •     信貸風險的外界市場指標的重大
           of credit risk, e.g. a significant increase in the credit                 惡化，例如信貸息差大幅增加、
           spread, the credit default swap prices for the debtor;                    債務人的信貸違約掉期價；

     •     existing or forecast adverse changes in business,                   •     預期將導致債務人履行其債務責
           financial or economic conditions that are expected to                     任的能力大幅下降的業務、財務
           cause a significant decrease in the debtor’s ability to                   或經濟狀況的現有或預測不利變
           meet its debt obligations;                                                動；

     •     an actual or expected significant deterioration in the              •     債務人經營業績的實際或預期重
           operating results of the debtor;                                          大惡化；

     •     an actual or expected significant adverse change in the             •     導致債務人履行其債務責任的能
           regulatory, economic, or technological environment of                     力大幅下降的債務人監管、經濟
           the debtor that results in a significant decrease in the                  或技術環境的實際或預期重大不
           debtor’s ability to meet its debt obligations.                            利變動。

     Irrespective of the outcome of the above assessment,                       無論上述評估結果如何，本集團假定
     the Group presumes that the credit risk has increased                      合約付款逾期超過 30 日時，信貸風險
     significantly since initial recognition when contractual                   自初始確認以來已大幅增加，除非本
     payments are more than 30 days past due, unless the                        集團有合理及可靠資料證明可予收回
     Group has reasonable and supportable information that                      則當別論。
     demonstrates otherwise.

     Despite the aforegoing, the Group assumes that the credit                  儘管有上述規定，若於各報告期末金
     risk on a financial instrument has not increased significantly             融工具被判定為具有較低信貸風險，
     since initial recognition if the debt instrument is determined             本集團會假設債務工具的信貸風險自
     to have low credit risk at the end of each reporting period.               首次確認以來並未顯著上升。在以下
     A financial instrument is determined to have low credit risk               情況下，金融工具會被判定為具有較
     if (i) it has a low risk of default, (ii) the borrower has a strong        低信貸風險：(i) 金融工具具有較低違
     capacity to meet its contractual cash flow obligations in the              約風險；(ii) 借款人有很強的能力履行
     near term and adverse changes in economic and business                     近期的合約現金流義務及經濟及商業
     conditions in the longer term may, but will not necessarily,               環境的長期不利變動有可能但未必會
     reduce the ability of the borrower to fulfil its contractual cash          降低借款人履行合約現金流義務的能
     flow obligations.                                                          力。

     For financial guarantee contracts, the date that the Group                 就財務擔保合約而言，本集團成為不
     becomes a party to the irrevocable commitment is considered                可撤銷承諾訂約方之日期被視為初始
     to be the date of initial recognition for the purposes of                  確認日期，以評估金融工具的減值。
     assessing the financial instrument for impairment. In                      於評估財務擔保合約之信貸風險自初
     assessing whether there has been a significant increase in                 步確認以來是否顯著增加時，本集團
     the credit risk since initial recognition of financial guarantee           會考慮特定債務人將就合約發生違約
     contracts, the Group considers the changes in the risk that                風險的變動。
     the specified debtor will default on the contract.

     The Group considers the following as constituting an event                 本集團認為以下情況就內部信貸風險
     of default for internal credit risk management purposes as                 管理目的而言構成違約事件，因為過
     historical experience indicates that receivables that meet                 往經驗表明符合以下任何一項條件的
     either of the following criteria are generally not recoverable:            應收款項一般無法收回：

     •     When there is a breach of financial covenants by the                 •     交易對手違反財務契諾；或
           counterparty; or

     •     Information developed internally or obtained from                    •     內部產生或獲取自外部來源的資
           external sources indicates that the debtor is unlikely to                  料表明，債務人不太可能向債權
           pay its creditors, including the Group, in full (without                   人（包括本集團）全額還款（未計
           taking into account any collaterals held by the Group).                    及本集團持有的任何抵押品）。

     The Group considers that default has occurred when the                     倘該工具已逾期超過 90 日，則本集團
     instrument is more than 90 days past due unless the Group                  認為已產生違約，除非本集團有合理
     has reasonable and supportable information to demonstrate                  及可靠資料證明較寬鬆的違約標準更
     that a more lagging default criterion is more appropriate.                 為適用則當別論。

     Financial assets (Continued)                                           金融資產（續）
     Impairment under ECL model (Continued)                                 預期信貸虧損模式項下減值（續）
     Measurement and recognition of ECL                                     預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
     The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability                預期信貸虧損的計量為違約概率、違
     of default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the              約損失率（即存在違約時的違約損失
     loss if there is a default) and the exposure at default. The           程度）及違約風險的函數。違約概率及
     assessment of the probability of default and loss given                違約損失率乃基於根據前瞻性資料調
     default is based on historical data adjusted by forward-looking        整的歷史數據評估。
     information.

     Generally, the ECL is estimated as the difference between              一般而言，預期信貸虧損估計為本集
     all contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in                團根據合約應收的所有合約現金流量
     accordance with the contract and all the cash flows that the           與本集團預計收取的所有現金流量之
     Group expects to receive, discounted at the effective interest         間的差額，並按初始確認時釐定的實
     rate determined at initial recognition.                                際利率貼現。

     For a financial guarantee contract, the Group is required to           就財務擔保合約而言，本集團須僅在
     make payments only in the event of a default by the debtor             債務人違反保證文書條款的情況下作
     in accordance with the terms of the instrument that is                 出付款。因此，預期虧損是償還持有
     guaranteed. Accordingly, the expected losses is the present            人信貸損失的預期付款的現值，該信
     value of the expected payments to reimburse the holder for             貸損失減去本集團預期從持有人、債
     a credit loss that it incurs less any amounts that the Group           務人或任何其他方獲得的任何金額。
     expects to receive from the holder, the debtors or any other
     party.

     For ECL on financial guarantee contracts for which the                 就無法釐定實際利率的財務擔保合約
     effective interest rate cannot be determined, the Group                的預期信貸虧損而言，本集團將採納
     will apply a discount rate that reflects the current market            反映目前對貨幣時間價值的市場評估
     assessment of the time value of money and the risks that are           及該現金流特定風險的貼現率，惟僅
     specific to the cash flows but only if, and to the extent that,        限於風險按調整貼現率而非調整所貼
     the risks are taken into account by adjusting the discount rate        現的現金不足納入考慮時。
     instead of adjusting the cash shortfalls being discounted.

     Interest income is calculated based on the gross carrying              利息收入根據金融資產的賬面值總額
     amount of the financial asset unless the financial asset is            計算，惟金融資產為信貸減值的情況
     credit impaired, in which case interest income is calculated           除外，於此情況下，利息收入根據金
     based on amortized cost of the financial asset.                        融資產的攤銷成本計算。

     Definition of default                                                 違約的定義
     For internal credit risk management, the Group considers an           就內部信貸風險管理而言，當內部產

     contractual terms.                                                    件。

     Group has reasonable and supportable information to                   理及可靠資料證明較寬鬆的違約標準
     demonstrate than a more lagging default criterion is more             更為適用則當別論。
     appropriate.

     Effective interest method                                                  實際利率法
     The effective interest method is a method of calculating the               實際利率法是一種計算相關報告期內

     income over the relevant reporting periods. The effective                  入的方法。實際利率為於金融資產的
     interest rate is the rate that exactly discounts estimated                 預期存續期或（如適用）更短期間內能

     received that form an integral part of the effective interest              構成實際利率不可或缺部分之一切已

     initial recognition.

     Impairment of financial assets                                             金融資產減值
     Financial assets, other than those at FVTPL, are assessed for              除按公允值計入損益者外，金融資產
     indicators of impairment at the end of each reporting period.              於各報告期末被評估是否有減值跡

     is objective evidence that, as a result of one or more events              未來現金流量因於初步確認金融資產
     that occurred after the initial recognition of the financial asset,        後發生的一項或多項事件而受到影響

     affected.

     decline in the fair value of that investment below its cost is             之公允值嚴重或長期低於其成本乃被
     considered to be objective evidence of impairment.                         視為減值之客觀證據。

     Financial liabilities and equity instruments                              金融負債及股本工具
     Debt and equity instruments issued by a group entity                      由集團實體發行的債務及股本工具乃
     are classified as either financial liabilities or as equity               根據合同安排的性質以及金融負債及
     in accordance with the substance of the contractual                       股本工具的定義分類為金融負債或股
     arrangements and the definitions of a financial liability and an          本。
     equity instrument.

     Equity instruments                                                        股本工具
     An equity instrument is any contract that evidences a residual            股本工具為證明實體扣除其所有負債
     interest in the assets of an entity after deducting all of its            後資產剩餘權益的任何合約。本公司
     liabilities. Equity instruments issued by the Company are                 發行的股本工具以已收所得款項扣除
     recognized at the proceeds received, net of direct issue                  直接發行成本後確認。
     costs.

     Repurchase of the Company’s own equity instruments is                     購回本公司本身的股本工具乃直接於
     recognized and deducted directly in equity. No gain or loss               權益確認及扣除。並無因購買、出售、
     is recognized in profit or loss on the purchase, sale, issue or           發行或註銷本公司本身的股本工具引
     cancellation of the Company’s own equity instruments.                     致的收益或虧損於損益內確認。

     Effective interest method                                                 實際利率法
     The effective interest method is a method of calculating the              實際利率法為計算金融負債攤銷成本
     amortized cost of a financial liability and of allocating interest        及於相關報告期間分配利息開支的方
     expense over the relevant reporting periods. The effective                法。實際利率乃按金融負債的預計年
     interest rate is the rate that exactly discounts estimated                期或適用的較短期間，將估計日後現
     future cash payments (including all fees and points paid or               金付款（包括構成實際利率不可或缺
     received that form an integral part of the effective interest             部份之一切已付或已收費用及點數、
     rate, transaction costs and other premiums or discounts)                  交易成本及其他溢價或折讓）確切貼
     through the expected life of the financial liability, or, where           現至初步確認時的賬面淨值的利率。
     appropriate, a shorter period to the net carrying amount                  利息開支按實際利率基準確認。
     on initial recognition. Interest expense is recognized on an
     effective interest basis.

     Financial liabilities at amortized cost                                   按攤銷成本計量的金融負債
     Financial liabilities (including trade and other payables,                金融負債（包括貿易及其他應付款項、
     amounts due to related parties and bank and other loans) are              應付關聯方款項以及銀行及其他貸款）
     subsequently measured at amortized cost, using the effective              其後採用實際利率法按攤銷成本計量。
     interest method.

     Derecognition                                                            終止確認
     The Group derecognizes a financial asset only when the                   本集團僅在自資產收取現金流量之權
     rights to receive cash flows from the assets expire or, when             利屆滿或其將金融資產及擁有有關資
     it transfers the financial assets and substantially all the risks        產的絕大部分風險及回報轉讓予另一
     and rewards of ownership of the assets to another entity. If             方時終止確認金融資產。倘本集團既
     the Group neither transfers nor retains substantially all the            無轉移亦無保留已轉移資產所有權的
     risks and rewards of ownership and continues to control the              絕大部分風險及回報，並繼續控制所
     transferred financial asset, the Group recognizes its retained           轉移金融資產，本集團將確認於該資
     interest in the asset and an associated liability for amounts it         產之保留權益及可能須支付之相關負
     may have to pay. If the Group retains substantially all the risks        債。倘本集團保留已轉讓金融資產所
     and rewards of ownership of a transferred financial asset,               有權的絕大部分風險及回報，本集團
     the Group continues to recognize the financial asset and                 繼續確認金融資產，並就已收取之所
     also recognizes a collateralized borrowing for the proceeds              得款項確認有抵押借款。
     received.

     On derecognition of a financial asset, the difference between            一旦終止確認金融資產，資產的賬面
     the asset’s carrying amount and the consideration received               值與已收及應收代價的差額會在損益
     and receivable is recognized in profit or loss.                          中確認。

     The Group derecognizes financial liabilities when, and only              本集團只有在責任獲免除、取消或終
     when, the Group’s obligations are discharged, cancelled or               止時，方會終止確認金融負債。獲終
     expire. The difference between the carrying amount of the                止確認之金融負債之賬面值與已付及
     financial liability derecognized and the consideration paid and          應付代價間差額會於損益確認。
     payable is recognized in profit or loss.

5.   CRITICAL ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENTS AND                                 5.   重大會計判斷及估計不確定性
     KEY SOURCES OF ESTIMATION UNCERTAINTY                                   的主要來源
     In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which            於應用附註 4 所載的本集團會計政策
     are described in note 4, the Directors are required to make             時，董事須就未能自其他來源清楚顯
     estimates and assumptions about the carrying amounts of                 示的資產及負債的賬面值作出估計及
     assets and liabilities that are not readily apparent from other         假設。估計及相關假設根據過往經驗
     sources. The estimates and underlying assumptions are                   及被認為相關的其他因素作出。實際
     based on historical experience and other factors that are               結果或與該等估計不符。
     considered to be relevant. Actual results may differ from
     these estimates.

     The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed                   估計及相關假設按持續經營基準審
     on an ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are              閱，就會計估計作出的修訂於對估計
     recognized in the period in which the estimates is revised if           作出修訂的期間內確認（倘有關修訂
     the revision affects only that period, or in the period of the          只影響該期間），或於修訂的期間及
     revision and future periods if the revision affects both current        日後期間確認（倘有關修訂影響當期
     and future periods.                                                     及日後期間）。

35. RESERVES                                                                            35. 儲備
     THE GROUP                                                                             本集團
     Share premium                                                                         股份溢價
     Under the Companies Law (Revised) of the Cayman Islands,                              根據開曼群島公司法（經修訂），計入
     amount included in the share premium account of the                                   本公司股份溢價賬中的金額可分派予
     Company is distributable to the shareholders of the Company                           本公司股東，前提是緊隨建議分派股
     provided that immediately following the date on which the                             息日期後本公司將有能力在日常業務
     dividend is proposed to be distributed, the Company will be in                        過程中償還到期債務。
     a position to pay off its debts as and when they fall due in the
     ordinary course of business.

     Merger reserve                                                                        合併儲備
     The merger reserve represents the difference between the                              合併儲備指本公司已發行股本面值與
     nominal value of the share capital issued by the Company and                          根據本集團重組所收購附屬公司合計
     the aggregate capital of the subsidiaries acquired pursuant                           資本之間的差額，根據合併會計基準
     to the Group Reorganization, accounted for as capital                                 作為最終控股公司的注資入賬。
     contribution from ultimate holding company under merger
     basis of accounting.

     government authorities are responsible for the entire pension     任。
     obligations payable to retired employees.

     The Group also operates a Mandatory Provident Fund                本集團亦根據香港強制性公積金計劃
     Scheme (“the MPF scheme”) under the Hong Kong                     條例為於香港僱傭條例的司法管轄權
     Mandatory Provident Fund Schemes Ordinance for                    下受聘用且過往並非受界定福利退休
     employees employed under the jurisdiction of the Hong Kong        計劃涵蓋的僱員，經營一項強制性公
     Employment Ordinance and not previously covered by the            積金計劃（「強積金計劃」）。強積金計
     defined benefit retirement plan. The MPF scheme is a defined      劃乃由獨立受託人管理的界定供款退
     contribution retirement plan administered by independent          休計劃。根據強積金計劃，僱主及其
     trustees. Under the MPF scheme, the employer and its              僱員各自須按僱員相關收入的 5% 向
     employees are each required to make contributions to the          計劃作出供款，每月相關收入上限為
     plan at 5% of the employees’ relevant income, subject to a        1,500 港元。計劃供款即時歸屬。
     cap of monthly relevant income of HKD1,500. Contributions
     to the plan vest immediately.

40. CAPITAL MANAGEMENT                                               40. 資本管理
   The Group’s primary objectives of managing capital are to           本集團管理資本的主要目的是維護其
   safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern,       持續經營的能力，從而使其能夠透過
   and to provide returns for shareholders and benefits for other      按合理成本取得融資，繼續為股東提
   stakeholders, by securing access to finance at a reasonable         供回報及為其他持份者提供利益。
   cost.

   The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages its            本集團積極及定期檢討和管理其資本
   capital structure to maintain a balance between the higher          架構，以在維持較高借款水平可能帶
   shareholder returns that might be possible with higher              來的高股東回報取得平衡，並因應不
   levels of borrowings, and makes adjustments to the capital          同的經濟狀況為資本架構作出調整。
   structure in light of changes in economic conditions.

   Based on recommendations of the board of directors,                 根據董事會建議，本集團將透過調整
   the Group will balance its overall capital structure through        向股東派付的股息金額、發行新股、
   adjusting the amount of dividends paid to shareholders,             籌集新債務融資或出售資產以減少債
   issuing of new shares, raising new debt financing or selling        務，藉以平衡其整體資本架構。於報
   assets to reduce debt. During the reporting period, there           告期內，並無更改目標、政策或程序。
   were no changes in the objectives, policies or processes.

   The Group monitors capital with reference to its debt position.     本集團透過參考其債務狀況監察資
   The Group’s strategy was to maintain the equity and debt in         本。本集團的策略為將權益與債項維
   a balanced position and ensure there was adequate working           持在一個平衡的位置，並確保擁有足
   capital to service its debt obligation.                             夠的營運資金償還其債務。

   Other price risk                                                     其他價格風險
   The Group is expose to equity price risk through its                 本集團因其於上市股本證券的投資而
   investments in listed equity securities. The Directors do not        面臨股本價格風險。董事並未實施具
   implement specific measurements to mitigate the price risk.          體措施以降低價格風險。倘上市股本
   If the market price of the listed equity securities increased or     證券的市價上升或下跌 5%，則本集
   decreased by 5%, the Group would recognize additional gains          團於截至 2018 年 12 月 31 日止年度確
   or losses of RMB2,551,000 for the year ended December 31,            認額外收益或虧損為人民幣 2,551,000
   2018(2017: RMB1,664,000).                                            元（2017 年：人民幣 1,664,000 元）。

   The Group’s commodity price risk is mainly the exposure to           本集團的商品價格風險主要為本集團
   fluctuations in the prevailing market price of coke, coal, and       購買、生產及銷售的主要商品焦炭、
   refined chemical products which are the major commodities            煤炭及精細化工產品的現行市價波動
   purchased, produced and sold by the Group. To minimize this          風險。為盡量降低此風險，本集團訂
   risk, the Group enters into future contracts to manage the           立期貨合約以管理本集團有關預測產

   forecasted purchase of raw materials and inventories.                險。公允值變動與焦炭、煤炭及精細

   The Directors are responsible for the Group’s cash                                    董事負責本集團的現金管理，包括現
   management, including the short term investment of                                    金盈餘的短期投資及籌集貸款以應付
   cash surpluses and the raising of loans to cover expected                             預期現金需求。本集團的政策為定期
   cash demands. The Group’s policy is to regularly monitor                              監察流動資金需求及遵守放款契諾，
   its liquidity requirements and its compliance with lending                            以確保其維持足夠現金儲備及來自主
   covenants, to ensure that it maintains sufficient reserves of                         要金融機構的充足承諾資金額度，以
   cash and adequate committed lines of funding from major                               應付其長短期的流動資金需求。
   financial institutions to meet its liquidity requirements in the
   short and longer term.
